- **scientific-sentence-framing**: Repair sentence architecture in scientific results prose so each sentence is easy to enter, easy to follow, and easy to connect to the next. Use when the text has the right information but the sentence order makes the reader work too hard. Puts a concrete subject first, keeps the number next to what it measures and next to its comparison group, puts familiar information before new, and avoids front-loaded modifiers and possessives. This is Skill 3 of the three-skill scientific-writing suite (apply after scientific-results-writing for tone and de-densify-scientific-prose for information load). ([laurenkolinger/manuscript_skills](https://github.com/laurenkolinger/manuscript_skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-sentence-framing))
